# 2020 Q3 H4 LOG

Append-only narrative log for Q3 (2020-07-01 through 2020-09-30), Lane A
(`HISTORICAL_MARKET_APPRENTICESHIP`), under `AI_TRADER_MARKET_APPRENTICESHIP_V1` and the newly
installed `AI_TRADER_Q3_Q4_OPERATING_STANDARD_V1.md`. Never edited except by appending — corrections
are logged as new ERRATUM entries, never rewritten in place.

### ADMINISTRATIVE (real-time) -- AI TRADER Q3/Q4 OPERATING STANDARD V1 installed, Q3 replay beginning

Direct CEO mandate installed: full text in `AI_TRADER_Q3_Q4_OPERATING_STANDARD_V1.md`. Summary of
what changes from this point forward:

- Q2's 66 trades (17 structured-comparable) are FROZEN as historical baseline -- not touched,
  not rescored, not renumbered. `TRADER_KNOWLEDGE_CHECKPOINT_2020_Q2.md` (STATUS: FINAL) stands
  unchanged as the permanent Q2 record.
- Q3 trade counter starts at 0. New trades use QUARTER_TRADE_ID (Q3-001, Q3-002, ...) as primary,
  LIFETIME_TRADE_ID (starting 67) as a secondary audit pointer. Quarterly statistics never mix
  cohorts.
- PIPS become the primary distance/result unit (0.10 price = 1 pip; 1.00 price = 10 pips). Raw
  points retained as secondary audit info only.
- TP Execution Buffer V1 continues, now pip-denominated (10 pips = 1.00 price point), TP-only,
  never applied to SL, never applied retrospectively to Q2/#66.
- Minimum acceptable TP1: >=1.50R after the buffer, or NO_TRADE. Never invent structure to hit a
  desired RR.
- Multi-Target System V1: TP1 (>=1.50R floor) / TP2 (guidance ~2.25-2.50R+) / TP3 (uncapped,
  genuine structure only) with default 40/30/30 position split, all frozen before entry,
  TARGET_MODE honestly set per trade (TP1_ONLY / TP1_TP2 / TP1_TP2_TP3) -- never fabricated to
  fill slots.
- Q2 knowledge (lessons, mistakes, playbooks, regime/alignment understanding) carries forward in
  full. Q2 specific trade OUTCOMES may not be peeked at to re-fit Q3 decisions -- forward-only
  learning, no retrospective optimization.

Q3 performance cohort: 2020-07-01 00:00:00 UTC through 2020-09-30 23:45:00 UTC. Starting now at
the first genuinely unseen bar, 2020-07-01 00:00:00 UTC. One-step-one-read, no lookahead, same
recovery/verification/close-based-execution/visualization conventions as Q2, continuing without
interruption.

### ERRATUM (real-time, self-discovered) -- Q3-PL-0002 bar/timestamp mislabeling, corrected

Q3-PL-0002 as originally written mislabeled its four bars: each OHLCV row was shifted one slot
against its timestamp, and the true 01:15 UTC bar's data was dropped entirely and replaced with a
garbled, non-existent duplicate row. Root cause: a copy/composition slip while writing the batch
entry, not a data-retrieval or replay error -- the underlying `data_get_ohlcv` reads and their
python3 timestamp verifications were all correct at the time each bar was fetched (each bar's
open exactly equals the prior bar's close, confirming the underlying sequence is intact); only the
narrative log entry mis-mapped them. No trade decision was affected -- FLAT throughout, no setup
was evaluated or missed based on the mislabeled entry.

### MATERIAL_EVENT (2020-07-01 10:15-10:30 UTC) -- developing SHORT candidate, entry withheld pending confirmation

10:15 O1787.762/H1787.82/L1785.204/C1786.132 V477(real) -- decisive down-close, breaks the London
range floor (~1785.6-1785.8 held since 08:00).
10:30 O1786.132/H1786.132/L1780.261/C1780.786 V878(real, largest of the Q3 session so far) --
sharp continuation lower, wide range (5.87pts).

Two consecutive real-volume down-closes technically forms the bare Playbook A-prime pattern. NOT
ENTERED, for explicit, evidence-grounded reasons:
1. Structurally this is a round-trip back to the Q3 session's own opening level (~1780.8, matching
   the pre-London-push lows from 03:00-04:00) -- not a break to a fresh low beyond anything already
   established this session. A round-trip-to-open is a weaker signal than a genuine fresh breakdown.
2. AI_TRADER_CONTEXT_V1 cross-scale check: H1 EMA(50) [confirmed] = 1776.929, price (1780.786)
   still well ABOVE it -- the medium-term H1 orientation has NOT confirmed a bearish re-alignment.
   This is exactly the staleness/cross-scale tripwire the indicator was built for, and it is
   flagging genuine uncertainty here, not confirmation.
3. Session VWAP (1786.228) was broken to the downside for the first time this session on this
   bar -- a real, fresh signal, but one data point.
4. Per TOC-003/TRADER_LESSON_021 (the apprenticeship's own strongest post-break discriminator),
   the outcome-relevant read is the reaction over the NEXT 1-2 bars -- immediate continuation vs.
   stall -- not the trigger bars themselves. Per the Q2 forensic review's central lesson (do not
   over-weight a directional read before checking whether H1/M15 has genuinely, currently
   re-aligned), entering directly on this bar's close would repeat the exact FULLY_ALIGNED-style
   pattern that underperformed all of Q2.

WATCHING FOR: a third bar continuing lower without stall (ideally closing below the H1 EMA(50)),
which would constitute genuine local bearish re-alignment per Playbook A-prime's actual standard --
OR a stall/reversal bar, which would classify this as TOC-003's stall signature and confirm NO_TRADE
was correct. Continuing one-step-one-read, full attention on this developing setup.

exactly the R10 CLEAN_BREAKOUT/PRICE_DISCOVERY scenario this project has repeatedly flagged as its
highest-caution zone (Q1's two extraordinary-volume episodes at 1709.7-1713.73 each failed to
resolve despite record volume -- extreme volume alone has never been shown to guarantee
resolution in this apprenticeship's own record).

Per AI_TRADER_Q3_Q4_OPERATING_STANDARD_V1.md §7/§10, a target must come from actual visible
structure, not be invented -- "price-discovery objective where justified" is a valid anchor in
principle, but nothing currently justifies a specific level here (no measured move, no prior
extension pattern, no round-number precedent established in this apprenticeship's own record).
**DECLINED**, despite this being the single strongest, most decisive real-volume move of Q3 so
far (V2352, the largest single-bar volume observed this quarter). Not a rejection of the thesis's
direction -- a rejection of entering without an identifiable, disciplined exit objective.

point (either a defended higher-low that itself becomes usable structure, or continued extension
that eventually stalls and reverses, providing an after-the-fact but still real level for a FUTURE
setup). Q3_TRADE_COUNT remains 2. FLAT. Continuing one-step-one-read.

1808.862 (V1084, CONTINUED down -- genuine 2-bar confirmation of the rejection)** -> 14:30 close
1806.895 (V822, continued lower).
ANALYSIS: this was a real, qualifying SHORT setup -- rejection off a genuine resistance zone near
the 1815.1 ICT level (wicks 1812.9-1813.472), confirmed by 2 consecutive down closes on rising
volume (V1710, V1084), matching the TOC-003 continuation signature. Using the fair decision point
(14:15 confirming close, 1808.862) against stop 1813.472 (real swing high) and TP1=1798.176: RR=
2.10R after buffer -- would have qualified.
HOWEVER: this session's 16-step batching cadence meant the confirming bar (14:15) was only read
AFTER the pointer had already advanced to 14:30, by which point actual executable price (1806.895)
gives RR=1.17R -- BELOW the 1.50R floor. Since replay_trade can only fill at the live current price,
not a retroactive historical price, entering now would be genuine chasing with insufficient
approved room. DECISION: NO_TRADE -- declined on real, current-price RR math, not fabricated.
PROCESS LESSON: batching 16 replay_steps before reading can cause a genuine signal to be missed
entirely during high-volume directional moves. Adopting smaller batches (4-8 steps) going forward
whenever volume is elevated/trending, to preserve the ability to catch and act on real confirmations
without chasing. Zero further Q3 trades (tally: 2 trades, 0W/2L, -2.204R).

stop convention were both followed correctly at entry).

### TRADE Q3-004 CLOSED (2020-07-17 12:15 UTC) -- LOSS
Close-based stop trigger: after grinding steadily back up from a low of 1795.118 (peak unrealized
~3.52R at one point) through London-into-NY session (11:45 wick high 1806.398, 12:00 wick high
1806.942 -- both touched above the 1806.513 stop but closed below, no trigger), the 12:15 UTC bar
(open 1806.378, high 1807.614, low 1806.246, close 1807.437, V233) closed above the stop. Fill =
that bar's own close (1807.437) -- worse than the nominal stop due to the bar's own extension
(consistent with the standing close-based-fill convention, same as Q3-003).
ENTRY: 1803.886 (2020-07-16 16:30 UTC) | EXIT: 1807.437 (2020-07-17 12:15 UTC)
RISK: 26.27 pips | RESULT: -35.51 pips | RESULT_R: -1.352R
This trade had strong initial confirmation (2-bar close-based break + ICT Displacement DN + bearish
H1 EMA/VWAP) and reached as low as 1795.118 (a peak unrealized RR of roughly 3.16R against the
26.27-pip risk) but was never actively managed with a trailing/partial-exit mechanism since
TARGET_MODE was TP1_ONLY and TP1 (1794.63 exec) was never actually touched by a close -- the closest
approach was the 19:45 UTC bar's low of 1795.118, about 5 pips short. Per standing discipline (BE
only after TP1 actually banked), no earlier exit was taken. Honest reflection for the playbook: a
high-RR setup that stalls just short of TP1 and then fully reverses is a real risk of the
TP1_ONLY/no-trailing approach -- worth considering partial structural trailing (already a standing
option per TP3 rules) on strong trades that show 2R+ unrealized without a second real level to bank
partial profit at, rather than only single-target holds.

### MATERIAL_EVENT (2020-08-10 13:30-13:44 UTC) [EXECUTION_FROZEN] -- PATTERN-007 CAND RESOLUTION
The 08-07 counterexample instance (Q3-P007-CAND-08-07-1344) has now reclaimed H1 EMA50: close
2039.452 (13:44:59, V2131) is above EMA50 (confirmed=2037.968). TIME_BELOW_EMA50 total: ~3 days
21 hours (13:44:59 on 08-07 to ~13:30 on 08-10). This does NOT change the frozen scoring --
per the active-falsification mandate, the instance was already correctly scored as a
COUNTEREXAMPLE the moment TIME_BELOW_EMA50 exceeded any prior reclaim timescale by a wide margin
(the longest prior instance was ~3.25hr; this one ran ~94x longer before reclaiming). The
eventual reclaim is itself a new data point worth recording: it shows the underlying uptrend
structure was NOT permanently broken by the NFP-driven decline -- price did eventually recover
above both the broken level and EMA50 -- but only after a multi-day process incomparable to the
fast mechanical reclaims (45min-3.25hr) that defined the pattern's first four instances. This
supports treating "genuine news-catalyst break" and "PATTERN-007 sweep" as two distinct
behaviors that can look similar at the moment of the break but resolve on entirely different
timescales -- exactly the discriminator hypothesis flagged when the counterexample was first
scored.

### MATERIAL_EVENT RESOLUTION (2020-08-16 22:14:59 UTC) [EXECUTION_FROZEN]
PATTERN-007 candidate Q3-P007-CAND-08-14-1659 RESOLVED. Structural level (1935.3) reclaimed
fast, within ~15min of the freeze (17:14:59, close=1935.97, "Displacement UP"). Price then
consolidated 1941-1946 (below EMA50) through the rest of Friday's session on declining
volume (1914->686->554->275->271->217->185), ending Friday at 20:59:59 close=1944.714 still
~1.3pt below EMA50. GAP-117 (weekend, ~49.25h, zero-price-gap verified) followed. First bar
after the Sunday 22:00 UTC reopen closed at 1947.818 -- immediately back above EMA50
(confirmed=1945.967) on the very first post-gap bar (V1561).
WALL_CLOCK_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 = 16:59:59 (08-14) to 22:14:59 (08-16) = ~53.25 hours.
ACTIVE_MARKET_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 (excluding the ~49.25h weekend closure) = ~4 hours
(16:59:59-20:59:59 Friday) + 0 (reclaimed on the first post-reopen bar) = ~4 hours total.
This is a NEW methodological finding: raw wall-clock TIME_BELOW_EMA50 can be substantially
inflated by weekend closures that contribute no actual trading time. In active-market terms
this instance behaved like a FAST reclaim (~4h), not a multi-day deviation, despite a
wall-clock duration (53.25h) that would otherwise look comparable to counterexample #1's
~2.5+ days. Cross-checking counterexample #1 (08-07): that instance also spanned a weekend
gap (GAP-112) but did NOT reclaim on the first post-reopen bar -- it remained below EMA50
for roughly another day of active trading after the Sunday reopen before eventually
reclaiming Monday 08-10 ~14:29:59. This confirms the weekend-inflation effect does not
explain counterexample #1's deviation (it stayed broken across genuine active trading too,
not just wall-clock weekend time), but it does mean this new instance's raw 53.25h figure
should NOT be read as comparably severe -- recommend tracking ACTIVE_MARKET_TIME_BELOW_EMA50
as a separate field going forward alongside wall-clock time.
SCORE: SUPPORT (fast reclaim in active-market terms, clean structural-level reclaim within
15min, EMA50 reclaim within ~4h of active trading).
PATTERN-007 tally: n=8 total (6 clean SUPPORT, 1 COUNTEREXAMPLE, 1 AMBIGUOUS). CONFIDENCE
remains MEDIUM, STATUS remains DEVELOPING_PATTERN. New open sub-question added:
ACTIVE_MARKET_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 vs WALL_CLOCK_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 should be tracked separately
for all future instances and retroactively estimated for existing ones where feasible.

period, price pushed higher decisively: close 06:59:59=1942.644 (V1697), 07:14:59=1944.46
(V1421), now above EMA50 (confirmed=1943.090). DID_RECLAIM_OCCUR = YES.
WALL_CLOCK_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 (freeze 14:59:59 08-19 to reclaim 07:29:59 08-24) = ~112.5
hours (~4.7 days) -- the longest wall-clock duration of any PATTERN-007 instance to date,
exceeding even counterexample #1.
ACTIVE_MARKET_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 (excluding GAP-120 [75min], GAP-121 [75min], and GAP-122
[49.25h weekend], all of which fell within this episode) = ~60.75 hours -- the
SECOND-longest active-market duration observed, behind only ambiguous case #2's ~69h, and
well within the same "severe, multi-day, no-clean-resolution" bracket as that instance
rather than the fast-reclaim cluster.
RECLAIM_VOLUME = 1421 (moderate, following a sustained low-volume basing period, then a
volume pickup 1155->1697 into the reclaim leg).
SCORE: given the severity of duration (second-longest active-market instance) and the fact
that price spent the vast majority of the episode consolidating/re-testing a wide range
(1911.586-1951.634) rather than cleanly trending back to EMA50, this is scored
**AMBIGUOUS**, not SUPPORT, per the same active-falsification discipline applied to
ambiguous case #2 -- DID_RECLAIM_OCCUR=YES but the practical "tradeable timeframe" claim of
PATTERN-007 is not supported by this instance either.
PATTERN-007 tally: n=10 total (7 clean SUPPORT, 1 COUNTEREXAMPLE, 2 AMBIGUOUS). CONFIDENCE
remains MEDIUM, STATUS remains DEVELOPING_PATTERN. This is now the THIRD severe-duration
instance (counterexample #1 ~2.5 days never fully resolving cleanly, ambiguous case #2
~69h, and this instance ~60.75h active-market / ~112.5h wall-clock) versus SEVEN clean
fast-reclaim instances (45min-6.25h) -- the bimodal distribution (fast OR very-slow, with
nothing in between) is becoming the pattern's most notable emergent property, though the
discriminator between the two regimes remains unidentified (no news catalyst present in
this instance, similar to ambiguous case #2; unlike counterexample #1 which had NFP).

### MATERIAL_EVENT RESOLUTION (2020-08-28 01:59:59 UTC) [EXECUTION_FROZEN]
PATTERN-007 candidate Q3-P007-CAND-08-27-1414 RESOLVED. Close 01:44:59=1932.42,
01:59:59=1935.802 (V1125), now above EMA50 (confirmed=1934.342). DID_RECLAIM_OCCUR = YES.
WALL_CLOCK_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 (freeze 14:14:59 08-27 to reclaim 01:59:59 08-28) = ~11.75h.
ACTIVE_MARKET_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 (excluding GAP-126, 75min) = ~10.5h.
This is the DIRECT test case for the news-catalyst discriminator hypothesis (this instance
had a confirmed major scheduled catalyst -- Fed Chair Powell's Jackson Hole speech). RESULT:
the hypothesis is NOT confirmed in its strong form -- despite record-shattering volume and
violent multi-directional whipsaws immediately around the news, this instance reclaimed in
~10.5h active-market time, comparable to the pattern's mid-range instances, NOT resembling
counterexample #1's multi-day non-reclaim following NFP. This is an important qualification:
news catalysts can produce extreme intrabar volatility without producing an extended
EMA50 deviation -- the two properties (volatility magnitude, deviation duration) appear to
be at least partially independent. Counterexample #1 (NFP, never reclaimed) and this
instance (Jackson Hole, reclaimed in ~10.5h) are both news-driven, yet resolved completely
differently -- suggesting the news-catalyst hypothesis in its original form does not
robustly discriminate; the type/content of the news event (a routine data release vs. a
regime-changing policy announcement) does not appear to determine duration in a
predictable way either, since Jackson Hole was arguably MORE consequential (a
policy-framework change) yet resolved faster than NFP.
SCORE: given ~10.5h active-market duration, this sits in the mid-range between the fast
cluster (45min-6.25h) and the severe cluster (44.5-69h) -- scored **SUPPORT** (reclaimed
within a plausibly "tradeable" timeframe, closer to the fast end of the spectrum than the
severe end), though flagged as NEWS-DRIVEN for future reference.
PATTERN-007 tally: n=12 total (8 clean SUPPORT including this one, 1 COUNTEREXAMPLE, 3
AMBIGUOUS). CONFIDENCE remains MEDIUM, STATUS remains DEVELOPING_PATTERN. The news-catalyst
discriminator hypothesis is now WEAKENED FURTHER: of the two confirmed-news-catalyst
instances observed (08-07 NFP counterexample, never reclaimed; 08-27 Jackson Hole, reclaimed
~10.5h), they produced opposite outcomes, meaning "has a news catalyst" alone does not
predict duration. What may matter more (untested, speculative) is whether the news
resolves a genuine uncertainty in the market's favor of the existing trend (Jackson Hole
was dollar-negative/gold-positive once digested, consistent with the broader advance) versus
against it (NFP strength reduces the case for further easing, arguably gold-negative,
consistent with the advance's context) -- but this is not yet a tested hypothesis, just an
observation worth tracking.

WALL_CLOCK_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 (freeze 11:29:59 09-02 to reclaim 01:59:59 09-07) = ~110.5h,
the longest wall-clock duration of any PATTERN-007 instance observed (exceeding the 08-19
instance's ~112.5h... actually just short of it -- 110.5h < 112.5h, so it is the
SECOND-longest wall-clock, not the longest; correcting the record-claim framing).
ACTIVE_MARKET_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 (excluding GAP-130 [75min], GAP-131 [75min], and GAP-132
[49.25h weekend], all within this episode) = ~58.75h.
CORRECTION: several progress entries during this episode (e.g. Q3-PL-0818, Q3-PL-0825)
prematurely claimed this instance was setting a new active-market duration record --
those claims were INACCURATE. The final computed ACTIVE_MARKET_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 (~58.75h)
does NOT exceed the 08-10 ambiguous case's ~69h record; it falls between the 08-24 instance
(~44.5h) and the 08-19 instance (~60.75h). This correction is logged explicitly per the
active-falsification mandate's requirement for honest tracking -- mid-episode duration
comparisons made before the reclaim is known are unreliable and should be treated as
provisional, not final.
RECLAIM_VOLUME = 744 (moderate).
SCORE: given the ~58.75h active-market duration (well within the severe-instance range,
between two prior ambiguous cases), this is scored **AMBIGUOUS**, consistent with the other
severe multi-day instances, not SUPPORT.
PATTERN-007 tally: n=16 total (11 clean SUPPORT, 1 COUNTEREXAMPLE, 4 AMBIGUOUS: 08-10 ~69h,
08-19 ~60.75h, 09-02 ~58.75h, 08-24 ~44.5h -- ranked by active-market duration). CONFIDENCE
remains MEDIUM, STATUS remains DEVELOPING_PATTERN. This instance also included a genuine
NFP news event (09-04) mid-episode that violently rejected an near-reclaim attempt without
producing a new counterexample-style non-reclaim -- consistent with the weakened
news-catalyst-as-sole-discriminator hypothesis.

CORRECTION (self-caught, ACTIVE_FALSIFICATION_V1): multiple progress entries during this episode
(Q3-PL-0886 through the GAP-133/floor-breach MATERIAL_EVENT entries) stated wall-clock-elapsed
figures that were ARITHMETICALLY WRONG -- they compounded an error that appears to have started
pre-compaction (Q3-PL-0886 claimed "~19hr elapsed" at 2020-09-07 15:59:59, when the true elapsed
from the 05:59:59 freeze was only 10hr), and I did not catch it until computing the final duration
via python3 datetime arithmetic at resolution. All intermediate "Xhr elapsed" / "approaching the
08-10 record" statements in this episode's log entries are INACCURATE and should be disregarded --
only this entry's computed figures are reliable.

VERIFIED via python3 datetime:
FREEZE = 2020-09-07T05:59:59Z, RECLAIM_CLOSE = 2020-09-08T16:14:59Z.
WALL_CLOCK_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 = 34.25h.
ACTIVE_MARKET_TIME_BELOW_EMA50 (excluding GAP-133 [5h Labor Day holiday gap], the only gap within
this episode) = 29.25h.

BREAK_QUALITY: close-based break below both EMA50 and the 1932.03 structural level, volume rising
into the break (342->1455).
Deepest excursion: low 1906.628 (2020-09-08 13:45-13:59 UTC), a THIRD breach of the 1907.066
structural floor (1st: 08-10 to 1871.748; 2nd: 08-24 to 1902.726) -- this breach was the
shallowest of the three (~0.44pt through) and on record volume (3986, later exceeded intrabar
context peak of ~2000-3500 through the episode).
RECLAIM_TIME/VOLUME: close-based reclaim after a sharp V-shaped bounce off the third floor breach;
volume on the reclaim bar 1720 (moderate, not a blow-off).
VOLUME_TREND: highly elevated throughout the deep-excursion phase (peaked 3986 on the breach bar),
moderating through the recovery leg.
DID_RECLAIM_OCCUR: YES.
INDEPENDENCE: overlaps partially with the Labor Day holiday period (GAP-133) but the deep
excursion and floor breach occurred entirely in normal LONDON/NY trading hours on 09-08, not
holiday-thinned liquidity -- no news-catalyst tag identified for this specific break.

SCORE: 29.25h active-market is well outside the "fast" SUPPORT band (45min-3.25h) but also well
below the four existing AMBIGUOUS instances' floor (44.5h). This is a genuinely NEW duration
point, filling in the continuous spectrum well below the previously-observed AMBIGUOUS cluster --
further undermining any clean bimodal split and supporting a truly continuous duration
distribution from ~15min to ~69h. Scored **AMBIGUOUS** (does not fit the fast-SUPPORT definition,
but did reclaim, unlike the COUNTEREXAMPLE).

PATTERN-007 tally: n=17 total (11 clean SUPPORT, 1 COUNTEREXAMPLE, 5 AMBIGUOUS: 08-10 ~69h,
08-19 ~60.75h, 09-02 ~58.75h, 08-24 ~44.5h, 09-07 ~29.25h -- ranked by active-market duration).
New standing lesson reinforced: mid-episode "hours elapsed" narration must be independently
re-verified via python3 at EVERY use, not carried forward/incremented by memory -- carrying an
error forward compounds it silently across many log entries.

(08-10, 1871.748) by ~22.9pt. The 1907.066 structural floor was breached SEVEN times within this
single episode (a new intra-episode record; previously each Q3-instance breached the floor at
most once).

unprecedented levels through the deepening (peak single-bar 7522, an all-time record for the

DID_RECLAIM_OCCUR=YES. No news catalyst identified at any point in the episode.

date on both duration and depth axes. Per the active-falsification mandate, this is NOT treated
as invalidating the pattern's core claim (it did eventually reclaim), but it substantially
extends both tails of the known distribution and should not be smoothed over as "just another

### METHODOLOGY INTEGRITY NOTE (2020-09-24 19:14:59 UTC) [EXECUTION_FROZEN]
Immediately after logging the Q3-P007-CAND-09-21-0844 RESOLUTION, six replay_step calls were issued
in a batch (per standing "quiet market -> wider batch" convention) without checking H1 EMA50
confirmed status between steps. On resuming per-bar review at pointer 19:14:59 UTC, study_values
showed close 1866.05 sitting well below H1 EMA50 confirmed=1874.976 -- i.e. price was NOT actually
quiet after the reclaim; it re-broke below EMA50 almost immediately. Reconstructing from the
revealed OHLCV: the reclaim bar (open 17:30, close 17:44:59 = 1876.358) held for exactly ONE bar --
the very next bar (open 17:45, close 17:59:59 = 1872.844) closed back BELOW the same-H1-window
EMA50 confirmed=1875.589. This is a genuine new break, but it was detected 5 bars (75 minutes) late
due to the batching lapse, not observed live at the moment it happened.

**Integrity disposition**: per the standing active-falsification requirement that a PATTERN-007
candidate must be pre-classified BEFORE its outcome is known, this instance's pre-classification
cannot be honestly certified as blind -- by the time it was recognized, 5 subsequent bars (through
19:00:00, close 1866.05, still unreclaimed) were already visible. This instance is therefore logged
as **Q3-P007-CAND-09-24-1759 [LATE-DETECTED / PRE-CLASSIFICATION COMPROMISED]** and will NOT be
added to the clean n=27 PATTERN-007 tally regardless of how it resolves, to preserve the tally's
integrity. Forward tracking from this point (19:14:59 UTC onward) IS genuinely blind and will be
logged normally; only the backward-looking classification is asterisked. Batch size reverts to
1-bar stepping for the remainder of this candidate's life.

**Substantive finding (independent of the integrity issue)**: this is the FASTEST failed-reclaim
ever observed in the Q3 record -- the EMA50 reclaim that ended the record-setting 77.25h/1848.842
episode held for only 15 minutes (one M15 bar) before price fell straight back below EMA50. This
"whipsaw reclaim" shape (reclaim -> immediate re-break) has not been seen elsewhere in the quarter
and is flagged as a new open question: does DID_RECLAIM_OCCUR=YES lose its meaning as a discrete
resolution event when the reclaim itself is this fragile? Noted for the next GOLD_BEHAVIOR_MODEL_V1
§7 re-synthesis; not acted on now (no redefinition of the already-scored 09-21 instance).

FREEZE (reconstructed) = 2020-09-24T17:59:59 UTC, close 1872.844, EMA50 confirmed=1875.589.
Current pointer 2020-09-24T19:14:59 UTC, close 1866.05, EMA50 confirmed=1874.976. Elapsed so far:
